    {
    		mesh = SMG/SMG|A2000,
    		texture = GunTexture/A2000_tex,
    		cullFace = None,

    		{
    			offset = -0.0300 0.0000 0.0000,
    			rotate = 0.0000 0.0000 0.0000,
    		}
    
    		attachment muzzle
    	{
    		offset = 0.0 0.402 0.027,
    		rotate = -90.0 0.0 -180.0,
    	}
